# Predictions ledger (protocol v2 §4)

One row per prediction made before a timed run. Rules: `research/architecture/measurement-protocol-v2.md` §4
(prediction card: build + base + hashes, driver, metric, baseline, closure check, term breakdown, predicted delta with
interval, falsification reading, decision). A model with **two misses** (outside its interval) is reviewed by its
author's team before it drives another build. Kept by mgmt-ops; owners send their prediction card before the run and
the measured result after it.

Columns: **id** · author · date (UTC) · build vs base · driver (S scroll / W window / M morph driver / G gesture /
F finger, §5.4) · metric · predicted [interval] · measured median [range or IQR] · in interval? · error ratio
(measured / predicted delta) · lesson.

Rows before 2026-10-01 ~10:45Z were made before protocol v2: their predictions were stated in messages or notes, not
on a prediction card, and the runs were n=3 (not 4v4). They are recorded so the two-miss rule counts them.

Readout per rotator tick (L11571 TimerFire): ≥10 ms whole-view Layout in the next frame, excluding ~0.1 ms carousel-rooted post-paint Layouts. Reading: C view + V1 gone (≥75 % of ticks no ≥10 ms view Layout, or carousel-rooted) -> W2 (img swap is the escape; direction fixed slots + src/transform rotation); C view + V1 view-rooted -> W3 or another tick dirtier, read rotoff; V2 #people-rooted at ≤ half C -> boundary holds; V2 view-rooted -> escape outside #people or no boundary on this WebKit; C not view-rooted -> stop. Prediction: V1 removes the ≥10 ms tick Layouts (W2); V2 #people-rooted ≤ half; headline ≥101 ms y≈1960 rebuild frame NOT tested here. Relabel: the 398x52 root (countdown 1 s tick) = fixed top nav `.start-selling-top-nav` (16,16)+scrollY, not a countdown strip. SECOND READ (mgmt-ops-5 16:3xZ): PENDING 3 items: (1) confound, does noswap keep the exit width steps (W3)? if not, "V1 gone" = W2 or W3, not separated; (2) grey zone: still view-rooted = ≥50 % of ticks, 50-75 % = MIXED (no direction, read rotoff), plus min ticks per run; (3) "idle dt>34 drops by the rotator share" needs a number + scoring runs or is info-only. Slot: right after P-032. 16:4xZ ANSWERS (fix-scroll-engine README 894dfbf): (1) none of the P14 builds has exit width steps (aw1-10e, re-keep, re-contain-js, st4-noswap all carry the layout-animations rewrite: avatars-exit opacity/translate/clip-path/transform, avatars-shift translate, avatars-pop; 0 width/flex-basis/margin keyframe hits; only the original baseline has width steps), so "W3 = exit width steps" was a mislabel; noswap also drops those animations (they match img.is-exit/.is-enter inside `ge`, never mounted). Reworded reading: V1 gone -> escape is in the swap path (`ge` mount/unmount: renderer insert/remove OR the animations' layer changes, NOT separated), direction = a rotator with no keyed remount (a split arm, `ge` mounted with animation:none, is a later block only if it matters); V1 view -> something noswap keeps (S/x+1 state writes, in-place src update, or a pending full layout), read rotoff.
